A court whose own offer is on the table is shown with its raw score ("11/50") beside "This peace carries" (FA-3, `settlement_baseline.py:2076-2095`). **Sightings:** conquest #12 (P3, turn 4, action 92, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0092.jpg`); conquest #17 (P3, turn 9, action 148, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0148.jpg`); the sea #18 (P2, turn 9, action 261, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0261.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The review says "no further land changes hands — each side keeps what it holds, now by treaty" and lists every province whose title the treaty fixes, France's own homeland held by another court first; a consented court's row reads "their own terms" without a /50 score. |
| **DDB-6** | P2 | FIXED October 14, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 1 (`6f360d03`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB6`; `_has_non_trivial_terms` skips the proposer side's own burden; the series arm S byte-identical; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB6` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **Easing the terms with France's own gold lowers the court's acceptance.** At the white-peace tier `_has_non_trivial_terms` counts ANY term with an amount, France's own payment included, and charges −10 "the terms claim a victory the field has not delivered" (`settlement_scoring.py:659-672`, `:784-787`), against the harshness function, which excludes proposer-paid clauses (`:722-755`). **Sightings:** conquest #20 (P3, turn 12, action 233, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0233.jpg`); diplomacy-first #19 (P2, turn 5, action 141, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0141.jpg`). **Decided fix:** `_has_non_trivial_terms` skips terms France pays; an Ease never lowers the eased court's score. |
| **DDB-7** | P2 | FIXED October 14, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 1 (`6f360d03`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB7`; `counsel_first` heads the list with the counsel's action and a running Cancel; the wizard cues "▼ more below"; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB7` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=ui_ux⟩ | **The Cabinet's recommended action sits below the fold with no scroll cue.** A court at OPEN_BORDERS lists Non-Aggression, Vassal, Declare War, Break Treaty, Downgrade, then Ultimatum and the four missions, Improve Relations among them (`diplomacy.py:12805-12827`); the wizard shows the first five above the fold (`diplomacy_wizard.gd:809-810`) with no cue that more follow; the cancel row of a running mission is below it too. **Sightings:** conquest #22 (P2, turn 14, action 260, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0260.jpg`); diplomacy-first #28 (P2, turn 10, action 239, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0239.jpg`); the sea #27 (P3, turn 19, action 394, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0394.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The counsel's recommended action and a running mission's Cancel stand first in the court's list; a list longer than its pane shows a scroll cue. |
| **DDB-8** | P2 | FIXED October 14, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 1 (`6f360d03`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB8`; the per-court peace refuses with `commitment_paradox`, the ally named, via `Utils.set_available`; the block text quotes the armistice's likelihood; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB8` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **A separate peace is offered, then blocked two screens later because an ally is still at war.** The per-court "Make peace with X only" / "Separate peace with X" eligibility never runs `get_peace_commitment_conflicts` (`settlement_staging.py:3108-3127`, `settlement_validation.py:363-524`); the next screen blocks the send as a HARD_STOP ("diplomatic contradiction": an ally at war with that court), and its text recommends an armistice without its own likelihood (`diplomatic_dialogue.py:1545-1590`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #3 (P3, turn 1, action 8, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0008.jpg`); the sea #15 (P2, turn 8, action 188, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0188.jpg`); the sea #16 (P2, turn 8, action 201, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0201.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The per-court button is disabled with the conflict's reason ("Bavaria is still at war with Austria") when a HARD_STOP stands; the block text quotes the armistice's own likelihood. |
| **DDB-9** | P2 | FIXED October 14, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 1 (`6f360d03`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB9`; the menu's sends are stamped by `can_sign(at_send=True)`; the popup renders `transient_error_display`; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB9` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **A proposal the Cabinet refuses to send fails silently.** "Satisfy their design" opens the generic proposal menu (`diplomatic_advisory.py:520-530`, `:1137-1141`); its "Propose peace" is refused by `can_sign(at_send)` (a paradox: an ally at war with that court, `treaty.py:438-448`), which re-attaches the dialogue with no DP spent; `main.gd:3727-3741` stores the reason as `transient_error_display`, which `proposal_confirm_popup.gd` `_build_content` never renders (`:389-478`), and the route returns before the error line prints. **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #5 (P2, turn 1, action 25, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0025.jpg`). **Decided fix:** `_build_content` renders `transient_error_display`; the menu's "Propose peace" is disabled with its reason under a paradox HARD_STOP. |
| **DDB-10** | P2 | FIXED October 14, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 1 (`6f360d03`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB10`; Recall sends its order; the priced bid passes the door; a refused line names its Cabinet control; the help and the Congress name "F1 → <court> → <row>"; the bid chips say a larger bid is typed; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB10` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=command⟩ | **The game teaches typed diplomacy and chips that write typed orders, then refuses them at the Cabinet door.** The client's Cabinet door (`main.gd:2586-2611`, `:2752-2759`, `:2804-2807`, `:2635-2656`) refuses any line naming a court or Talleyrand that is not an exempt word ("Matters of state are conducted at the table, Sire"), while the help's COMPACTS section (`meta_executor.py:1081-1094`) and the Congress table (`congress.py:1512`, `:1543`; `diplomatic_ledger.gd:2017-2032`) teach "buy off …", and the rail's Recall chip writes "Talleyrand, cancel mission with Prussia" into the command line (`notification_bar.gd:490-505`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #15 (P3, turn 4, action 105, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0105.jpg`); diplomacy-first #25 (P2, turn 8, action 192, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0192.jpg`); diplomacy-first #27 (P3, turn 10, action 229, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0229.jpg`); diplomacy-first #28 (P2, turn 10, action 239, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0239.jpg`); the sea #23 (P3, turn 12, action 312, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0312.jpg`). **Decided fix:** Every chip that acts on the Cabinet sends its structured order (Recall sends `cancel_mission`); the help and the Congress table name the Cabinet control ("F1 → <court> → Buy off their design") instead of a typed line; the typed bid the rail itself echoes ("bid for Sardinia, 1500 gold") passes the door — a priced order with its amount; the rail's and the Cabinet's chips bid 100g and say a larger bid is typed (`ai_diplomacy.py:111`, `:3503-3504`; `main.gd:2608-2610`). |
| **DDB-11** | P2 | FIXED October 14, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 1 (`6f360d03`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB11`; the chip reads "Separate peace with <court> — alone: N (band)", scored as the hand-off scores it; with DDB-X5 the two agree; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB11` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **The settlement table says a court "Will sign (55/50)"; the separate peace it hands off scores the same terms 0.** The table scores the joint package with `calculate_common_peace_acceptance` (`settlement_scoring.py:2148`); the per-court chip hands off to a bilateral preview that rebuilds the court's share as bilateral demands (a client-state carve becomes a demand weighted by provinces; gold per turn a lump sum) and scores it with the bilateral formula and its own harshness bands (`settlement_actions.py:3156-3276`, `:3053-3153`; `diplomacy.py:4758-4760`, `:4615-4627`). **Sightings:** the sea #15 (P2, turn 8, action 188, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0188.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The per-court chip shows the bilateral estimate it hands off to, scored by the same preview. |
| **DDB-12** | P2 | FIXED October 14, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 1 (`6f360d03`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB12`; `homeland_returns` titles a co-belligerent's retaken homeland home at the signature and in the forecast; the series arm H byte-identical; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB12` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **A peace titles a French homeland province to the ally who retook it, unannounced.** Bavaria retook Franche-Comté from Austria: the conquest is recorded from Austria, holder Bavaria (`world_state.py:4812-4815`); on signing, the status quo titles ground captured from the ceder to the holder's side, and an ally is its own side (`game_end.py:832-869`, `:1006-1011`), so France's homeland province went to Bavaria. **Sightings:** the sea #18 (P2, turn 9, action 261, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0261.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A co-belligerent's home province retaken by an ally is titled back to its home nation; the review lists it. |
| **DDB-13** | P2 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB13`; every response carries `gold_projected_net` (`ledger.chest_forecast`); the Gold counter reads "432 (−486 next turn)"; the end-turn gate stops once for a treasury about to go negative; the muster's `materiel_note` quotes the bill; the battle's [Materiel] line rides directly under the battle, naming the chest after it; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB13` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=economy⟩ | **The treasury goes negative at End Turn with no warning; a battle's materiel is charged unannounced.** Nothing projects next turn's net before End Turn: the Gold counter shows the treasury only (`main.gd:5593-5595`), the end-turn gate checks only envoys and last stands (`:2177-2192`), the projected Net lives in the ledger (`strategic_ledger.gd:818`); the materiel bill is charged at each battle, outside Net (`combat_executor.py:4329-4357`). **Sightings:** conquest #5 (P2, turn 2, action 21, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0021.jpg`); the sea #7 (P2, turn 1, action 28, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0028.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The Gold counter carries the projected net ("432 (−486 next turn)"); the end-turn gate adds a line when the treasury will go negative; a battle's reply names its materiel bill in the result's first lines; the muster quotes the battle's expected materiel bill beside its supply cost (sea #7: −425g took the treasury from 169 to −256). |
| **DDB-14** | P2 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB14`; `field_on_peace_soil` refuses the player's lead a field on peace soil, pre-validated before the objection (found by this row's own pins: the objection fired first); an army on its own court's soil is a declaration of war on the war-purpose road, not a third party's field (`enemy_nation`, found by the hook); a joiner's muster line says "— and will stand at Swabia" / "returns to <origin> if the works hold"; `returns_reinforcers_from_works` sends the joiners home at the works halt. GR5's named difference: the AI's lead keeps PT-F1 (SYSTEMS_REFERENCE §117); the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB14` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=combat_legibility⟩ | **Corps that march to the guns stay in the battle's province, and the muster never says so.** Every arriving reinforcer's location is set to the battle province before the battle (`combat_executor.py:7708-7724`); on a win they stay, and Gate 4 returns them only on a non-win (`:9127-9155`); when the lead halts before a province's works (RS-3, `:9229-9235`) the supporters already stand inside it beside the unbroken garrison. The muster says only "marches to the sound of the guns" (`display_names.py:812`, `:816`). **Sightings:** conquest #7 (P2, turn 3, action 56, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0056.jpg`); conquest #15 (P2, turn 6, action 112, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0112.jpg`); conquest #19 (P3, turn 12, action 218, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0218.jpg`). **Decided fix:** When the lead halts before the works, the arrived reinforcers return to their origins as Gate 4 returns them; the muster line says where they will stand after the battle ("and will stand at Tyrol"); a field on soil France is at peace with is refused to the lead as it is to his supporters (conquest #15: Massena alone was let into Austrian Moravia while the corps beside him were refused it, `combat_executor.py:1795-1801`, `:7693-7699`). |
| **DDB-15** | P2 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB15`; `evaluate_cautious` returns NONE when `attack_engages` finds nothing to fight on an enemy's province; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB15` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=marshal_drama⟩ | **A marshal objects "the enemy is too strong" to a garrison that is about to give way.** Once a garrison will give way (a detachment under 500, a capital under 5,000) `attack_engages` returns (None, None) (`objection_v2.py:482-503`), `get_target_intel_level` then reads UNKNOWN, which is ConcernLevel.STRONG (`:1419-1426`), and the cautious line fires against a walk-in — one action after the game printed "Vienna lies undefended — an attack takes it". **Sightings:** conquest #18 (P2, turn 10, action 187, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0187.jpg`); conquest #23 (P3, turn 14, action 265, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0265.jpg`). **Decided fix:** An attack on a province whose garrison does not fight raises no strength concern. |
| **DDB-16** | P2 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB16`; `last_stand_odds_line` names each road's chance on the card; `last_stand_fate` (fallen / escaped / captured) rides the answer and prints first, no peeked earlier diorama; the desk's roster carries our prisoners and the answer says "a prisoner of Austria, held at Vienna since turn 3"; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB16` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=marshal_drama⟩ | **A cornered marshal's fate is never told, and "where is <prisoner>" shrugs.** The last-stand card names no odds (`combat_executor.py:5151-5156`; the breakout chance is MARSHAL_FATE_ESCAPE_CHANCE − LAST_STAND_BREAKOUT_PENALTY, `strategic.py:1964-1968`); after Breakout or Fight to the Last no result line reached the window for Lannes, Ney or Murat (Massena's failed breakout did print); the question desk's roster is live marshals plus tombstones, so a prisoner is in neither and gets the shrug (`question_desk.py:2282-2289`, `llm_client.py:3185-3193`). **Sightings:** conquest #21 (P2, turn 13, action 239, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0239.jpg`); conquest #26 (P2, turn 16, action 300, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0300.jpg`); conquest #34 (P2, turn 20, action 363, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0363.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The card names each road's chance; the choice's result prints in the window (escaped / captured / fallen) on every road; the desk answers a prisoner ("held by Russia at Vilna since turn 16"). |
| **DDB-17** | P2 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB17`; the card says "charges ALONE — no corps joins a charge"; `_generate_description` chooses the line in the outcome's register (one draw either way); the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB17` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=combat_legibility⟩ | **The Glorious Charge goes in alone and the card never says so.** The charge calls `resolve_battle` for the charger alone (`combat_executor.py:10645-10650`): no muster, no joiners; the card (`glorious_charge_dialog.gd:86-90`) does not say so; "executes a brilliant maneuver!" is picked from the attacker's dice before the outcome (`combat.py:1305-1314`). **Sightings:** conquest #29 (P2, turn 17, action 319, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0319.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The card says "charges ALONE — no corps joins a charge"; the narrative line is chosen after the outcome. |
| **DDB-18** | P2 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB18`; `counsel._hopeless_attack` drops an attack the objection calls a suicide mission (≥ 5:1, read fog-honestly at the muster's totals); the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB18` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=command⟩ | **Berthier's "what I can do" suggests a hopeless attack.** The counsel offers the first legal attack pair regardless of odds (`counsel.py:171-191`, `:262-266`: "the odds are the muster's business") — the Emperor's few hundred men against 10,000. **Sightings:** conquest #34 (P2, turn 20, action 363, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0363.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The counsel drops an attack whose muster band would be hopeless. |
| **DDB-19** | P2 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB19`; `objection_v2.muster_expected_totals` — the attack objection (`_get_attack_odds_ratio`, `personality._get_strength_ratio`) reads the muster's committed totals at FULL / PARTIAL; pure (the hint latch put back); the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB19` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=marshal_drama⟩ | **An objection's odds and the muster's band disagree on the same attack.** The attack objection reads the marshal's own corps against one enemy marshal (`personality.py:295-305`, `:388-411`; `disobedience.py:605-607`); the muster counts every corps in reach (102k vs 52k). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #9 (P2, turn 2, action 64, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0064.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The attack objection reads the muster's expected totals. |
| **DDB-20** | P2 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB20`; `strategic.support_stands_at_approach`: a SUPPORT whose next step is the ally's contested field stands at the approach, order standing, at the order's issue and at the turn's tick; SUPPORT on the same field is exempt from the engagement gate; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB20` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=combat_legibility⟩ | **The muster's "order him to support" cannot be carried out.** A literal marshal's SUPPORT whose first step meets the enemy in the supported man's contested province hits the "reached the approach" branch, which clears the order and charges 1 AP (`strategic_executor.py:2630-2660`), so the next muster again reads him as "awaits explicit orders"; and a marshal on the battle's own field is engaged, so the strategic gate refuses his SUPPORT outright (`strategic_executor.py:813-841`) while the muster advises it (`display_names.py:808-810`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #11 (P2, turn 2, action 75, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0075.jpg`); diplomacy-first #14 (P3, turn 3, action 94, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0094.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A SUPPORT whose destination is the supported marshal's contested province stays standing and joins his next attack as a reinforcement; SUPPORT of a marshal in the same province is exempt from the engagement gate. |
| **DDB-21** | P2 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-6 (the sea); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_sea.py::TestDDB21` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=naval⟩ | **A corps standing in a province the peace just titled to France is "stranded" and faces internment.** The home zone is a land path from Paris (`withdrawal.py:354-392`, `:445-461`), so Oudinot in Munster (French by the treaty) is stranded and must walk home through Britain in two turns, while the embark rule treats the same ground as home soil that needs a dockyard (`naval_executor.py:402-406`). **Sightings:** the sea #21 (P2, turn 11, action 302, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0302.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A corps on a province France holds by title is home; a corps outside the home zone may embark from its own coastal province. |
| **DDB-22** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB22`; the harsher per-court dial reads "Demand more of <court>"; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB22` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **"Press <court>" means a separate peace in the advice and harsher terms on the button.** `settlement_staging.py:2455` ("Press {court} alone: the separate peace") against the per-court button "Press {court}" = `settlement_dial_harsher` (`:3375-3382`). **Sightings:** conquest #11 (P3, turn 4, action 87, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0087.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The button reads "Demand more of <court>". |
| **DDB-23** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB23`; `display_names.stream_term_phrase` names every stream's term ("for as long as the treaty stands", or its turns); the treaty itself stands first; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB23` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **A per-turn payment or action is offered with no duration.** "More generous" adds `gold_per_turn` (or, a round later, `ap_per_turn`) with no `turns` — bilateral streams are perpetual by design — and the display template has no duration slot (`diplomatic_templates.py:4504-4517`, `:4736-4740`; `diplomatic_executor.py:5320-5337`); on an armistice it replaces the armistice line in the term list. **Sightings:** conquest #31 (P3, turn 18, action 345, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0345.jpg`); diplomacy-first #10 (P3, turn 2, action 70, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0070.jpg`); diplomacy-first #20 (P3, turn 5, action 151, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0151.jpg`). **Decided fix:** Every stream's line names its term ("for as long as the treaty stands", or its turns); a generous round on an armistice keeps the armistice as the first listed term. |
| **DDB-24** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB24`; `settlement_presentation.side_court_label` names the sides by court ("France" / "The Third Coalition"); a court's white peace says it settles the whole war and names the per-court road; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB24` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **The war's sides read France as attacker while every war purpose reads "Defense".** France is the attacker of record in `starting_wars` (`europe_1805.json:66-73`), so the war instance lists France as Attackers / war leader, while the boot gives every belligerent a defense purpose (`world_state.py:10259-10275`); a white peace from a court's page stages the whole-war settlement on purpose (`diplomatic_executor.py:3505-3515`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #2 (P3, turn 1, action 6, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0006.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The sides are labelled by court ("France" / "The Third Coalition"), never attacker/defender; the white peace from a court's page says it settles the whole war, beside the per-court road. |
| **DDB-25** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB25`; `assess_terms` opens the feasibility card on Talleyrand's suggested terms with their acceptance ("Propose these terms"); the strength line says who outmatches whom; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB25` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **"What terms would they accept?" answers with no terms.** The button runs `expand_to_proposal` with no proposal type and lands on the generic standstill menu: no terms, no acceptance (`diplomatic_advisory.py:1296-1301`, `:1953-1962`; `diplomatic_executor.py:5980-6007`); "overmatched" is the bottom of the strength ladder inside "Their military strength is {X} relative to ours" (`diplomatic_templates.py:741-765`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #4 (P3, turn 1, action 12, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0012.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The button opens the feasibility card (the suggested terms with their acceptance); the strength line reads "we outmatch them" / "they outmatch us". |
| **DDB-26** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB26`; the band promises a counter only when the generator's dry run builds one (`counter_aware_likelihood`); the preview is titled by its treaty, the treaty first, the armistice's mechanics above the score; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB26` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **The armistice preview's title, band word and term list disagree with the court's page.** The page word is a score band ("Doubtful — may counter", `diplomacy.py:12096-12097`) while the preview runs the real counter generator ("no workable counter", `:4927-4970`); any war-state preview is titled "PEACE PREVIEW" and lists only clauses, never the treaty itself (`proposal_confirm_popup.gd:186-187`); the armistice's mechanics block renders last (`:1477-1490`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #10 (P3, turn 2, action 70, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0070.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The preview is titled by its treaty type and lists the treaty as its first term; the band word drops "may counter" unless a counter is constructible; the mechanics block stands above the score. |
| **DDB-27** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB27`; the assessment reads the war score live (`live_war_score_for`); the battlefield line only while a victory is wanting; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB27` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **Talleyrand's assessment ignores the battles fought since the last end turn.** `get_assessment_text` reads the stored `war_scores`, recalculated only at end turn (`diplomacy.py:12166-12173`, `:3820-3835`); battles only append records (`:10393-10405`), while the war panel computes the score live (`war_status.py:153-157`); `:13738` is the unconditional fallback line. **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #12 (P3, turn 2, action 80, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0080.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The assessment reads `calculate_war_score` live; the fallback line is gated on the score. |
| **DDB-28** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB28`; the review's petition line carries Grant / Decline (`settlement_answer_petition` → `answer_ally_petition_from_table`) on the staged draft; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB28` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=vassals⟩ | **An ally's petition over the settlement arrives only after the settlement is ratified.** Staging the settlement queues the ally's petition behind the settlement dialogue; the review shows it as a line with no control (`proposal_confirm_popup.gd:582-594`); it becomes current only after ratification, when the draft is gone, so Grant is refused ("The draft has moved on", `settlement_offers.py:1686-1687`, `settlement_staging.py:4125-4140`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #17 (P3, turn 4, action 131, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0131.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The review's petition line carries Grant / Decline acting on the staged draft. |
| **DDB-29** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB29`; `settlement_staging.purge_drafts_naming` at `set_diplomatic_state`: a court that leaves the war leaves no draft naming it; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB29` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **A ratified separate peace is followed by "your unratified draft … was set aside".** Ratification discards only the draft under its own scope key; the earlier whole-war draft naming the same court survives and is set aside at turn end with its court's name (`world_state.py:11235-11287`, `settlement_ratify.py:1701`, `settlement_staging.py:2617-2634`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #18 (P3, turn 5, action 133, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0133.jpg`). **Decided fix:** Ratification purges every scoped draft naming a court that just left the war. |
| **DDB-30** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB30`; "Less generous" restores exactly the previous round (`context["previous_round"]`), absent on round 1; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB30` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **A generous round cannot be stepped back.** The rebuilt options after "More generous" are Send / Even more generous / Not Now (`diplomatic_executor.py:5395-5410`): no way back to the previous round's terms. **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #20 (P3, turn 5, action 151, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0151.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A "Less generous" option carries the previous round's terms. |
| **DDB-31** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB31`; the buy-off chip names the design that wakes next and its price (`instruments.next_design_after_buyoff`, a counterfactual put back exactly); the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB31` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=agendas⟩ | **A bought-off design is followed at once by the next one, unannounced.** The buy-off chip names only the design it buys; `congress._buy_off_chain` (`congress.py:1515-1543`) knows the court's next design and its price (`diplomacy.py:13128-13135`) but the chip never says so. **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #27 (P3, turn 10, action 229, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0229.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The buy-off chip names the design that wakes next and its price. |
| **DDB-32** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB32`; the Treaties book lists the 1805 alliances: "an alliance of 1805, not a treaty of ours"; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB32` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **The ledger's Treaties book reads "No active treaties" while the Cabinet counts the 1805 alliances.** The TREATIES tab lists only `world.active_treaties` records (`diplomatic_ledger.py:890-894`, `diplomatic_ledger.gd:648-663`); the Cabinet's "Treaties (N)" is built from the diplomatic states, where ALLIANCE counts (`main.py:6979-7027`); the 1805 alliances (Spain, Bavaria) carry no treaty record. **Sightings:** the sea #3 (P3, turn 1, action 17, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0017.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The Treaties book lists the record-less 1805 alliances with the line "an alliance of 1805, not a treaty of ours". |
| **DDB-33** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB33`; Ease stops at the scorer's gold ceiling (1,000) and says so; the promised-region check counts a client state's provinces; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB33` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **Easing past 1,000 gold buys nothing, and then offers a province already promised to a new client.** Ease raises gold toward a 1,500 cap while the scorer stops crediting gold at 1,000 (`settlement_staging.py:1352-1356` vs `settlement_scoring.py:545-546`, `:1472-1474`); at the cap the dial offers France-held land, and its promised-region check counts only cede clauses, not the provinces of the "Erect Ireland" clause (`settlement_baseline.py:240-255`), so the validator refuses the draft (`carve_region_double_promised`, `settlement_validation.py:1312-1337`). **Sightings:** the sea #17 (P3, turn 8, action 235, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0235.jpg`). **Decided fix:** Ease stops raising gold where the scorer stops counting it, and says so; the promised-region check counts a client state's provinces. |
| **DDB-34** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB34`; the dispatch names "F1 → <court> → Bid for Their Allegiance"; the advice menu offers the bid while an auction is open; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB34` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=ai_aliveness⟩ | **The dispatch announces an allegiance auction for three turns and never says where to bid.** The dispatch line names no control (`dispatch.py:6880-6883`); Talleyrand's "What should we do about …" is a fixed peace-time template with no bid (`diplomatic_templates.py:770-796`); the Cabinet's page does carry "Bid for Their Allegiance" (`diplomacy.py:12924`, `:12976-13006`). **Sightings:** the sea #22 (P3, turn 12, action 308, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0308.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The dispatch line names the Cabinet chip; the advice menu offers the bid while an auction is open. |
| **DDB-35** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`33643c45`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB35`; Propose Peace stands in the WAR state behind the ONE verdict's paradox; the one-on-one refusal names the court's own controls; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_table.py::TestDDB35` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **A one-on-one war is told to "use bilateral peace", and the court's page offers none.** In the WAR state the Cabinet offers only Propose Armistice; Propose Peace exists only in ARMISTICE (`diplomacy.py:12649-12772`), while the refusal (`display_names.py:954`) and the war panel's "Negotiate Peace" point to it (`war_detail_popup.gd:652-661`); the game can stage a peace straight from war (`settlement_actions.py:3202-3212`). **Sightings:** the sea #24 (P3, turn 14, action 330, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0330.jpg`). **Decided fix:** Propose Peace stands in the WAR state, with the ally-at-war hard stop already used in ARMISTICE. |
| **DDB-36** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB36`; the header reads "The band weighs more than the men" only when the folded band reads worse than the raw numbers' band, else "Standing modifiers, already in the band"; the cautious line gives "his own 26k against 60k"; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB36` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=combat_legibility⟩ | **The muster's posture note reads against its own favourable band.** `_posture_note` opens with "The band weighs more than the men" whenever any modifier hurts, even when the band reads favourable; the cautious −10% is priced on the marshal's SOLO ratio, deliberately (PT-D1) (`combat_executor.py:1889-1912`). **Sightings:** conquest #3 (P3, turn 1, action 14, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0014.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The header prints only when the band is worse than the raw numbers' band; the cautious line gives the solo figures ("his own 26k against 34k"). |
| **DDB-37** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB37`; the battle event carries `advanced_to` and the enemy phase says "— and advances into X"; `_BY_RULE_NO_SHOWS` (literal_personality, neutral_soil) skip the "Where was" bank; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB37` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=combat_legibility⟩ | **The enemy phase never says a winner advanced, and asks "Where was Soult?" of a corps obeying its order.** A winning AI attacker's advance exists only in the prose message; the dialog builds its lines from structured fields and the battle event has no advance field (`enemy_phase_dialog.gd:198-205`, `:337-346`; `combat_executor.py:9260-9276`); a literal marshal with no SUPPORT order is recorded as a failed reinforcement and the failure bank prints "Where was Soult?" (`combat_executor.py:2980-3009`, `battle_report.py:923-965`). **Sightings:** conquest #4 (P3, turn 2, action 25, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0025.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The battle event carries `advanced_to` and the line says "and advances into Franconia"; the "Where was" bank skips by-rule no-shows. |
| **DDB-38** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB38`; `forced_retreat_clears_counter_punch` at every forced retreat (its rail row dismissed); `derive_register` reads a stalemate's field (routed / province lost = defeat; enemy routed / province taken = triumph); the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB38` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=combat_legibility⟩ | **A "stalemate" can rout the defender: the free-attack card and the "bloody stalemate" banner then lie.** The morale rout is gated only on "not victor" (`combat_executor.py:8360-8364`), so a stalemate can force the defender out, and the attacker then pursues into the emptied province (`:9194-9204`); a cautious defender earns the counter-punch on a stalemate (`combat.py:878-882`) and the normal forced-retreat branch never clears it (`:5512-5551`); the diorama's register reads only the outcome string (`battle_diorama.py:302-320`). **Sightings:** conquest #6 (P3, turn 2, action 35, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0035.jpg`); conquest #28 (P3, turn 17, action 316, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0316.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A forced retreat clears the counter-punch; the diorama's register reads the field (routed / province lost = defeat; enemy routed / province taken = triumph). |
| **DDB-39** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB39`; "Morale dropped" only when it fell, else "Morale is still low at N%"; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB39` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=economy⟩ | **"Morale dropped to 26%" right after morale rose from 21% to 26%.** The warning fires whenever new morale is under 40 and always says "dropped" (`economy_executor.py:1797-1802`). **Sightings:** conquest #14 (P3, turn 5, action 102, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0102.jpg`). **Decided fix:** "dropped" only when it fell; otherwise "is still low at 26%". |
| **DDB-40** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB40`; the province note prices fortify from the stance ("2 AP from a neutral stance — 1 to stand defensive, 1 to fortify"); the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB40` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=command⟩ | **The battle's advice quotes fortify at 1 AP; from a neutral stance it costs 2.** The muster note hardcodes "fortify him (1 AP)" (`combat_executor.py:2402-2406`); fortify from NEUTRAL auto-shifts to DEFENSIVE first (`tactical_executor.py:551-566`). **Sightings:** conquest #16 (P3, turn 6, action 115, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0115.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The note prices fortify from the marshal's stance, as `counsel.py:318-320` does. |
| **DDB-41** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB41`; "Cancel the Order (no cost)" on the objection dialog; `cancel` / `withdraw` clear a tactical or strategic objection with no trust, action or authority spent (`objection_withdrawn` on the response); the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB41` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=ui_ux⟩ | **An objection offers no plain cancel: every answer spends trust or an order.** The objection dialog carries three answers — Proceed as Ordered, Trust <marshal>'s Judgment, Compromise — each priced in trust or AP, and no way to withdraw the order (`objection_dialog.gd:178-196`, `:330-340`). **Sightings:** conquest #23 (P3, turn 14, action 265, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0265.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A Cancel answer withdraws the order at no cost. |
| **DDB-42** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB42`; `capture_recovers_title` (a titled or retaken province adds no `region_capture`) and `battle_win_on_own_soil` (no `battle_win` / `decisive_victory` on a field the victor held BEFORE the battle — a field the win itself conquered is not home soil; war exhaustion and the coalition shock stand); the result's [Alarm] line names each alarm it adds. The series arms B and G byte-identical (B reached Austria's two own-soil wins); the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB42` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=living_balance⟩ | **Europe's alarm rises when France fights on, or retakes, its own soil.** A capture adds +2 threat for any province the capturer did not hold at the START of the campaign, so provinces titled to France by treaty count as conquest every time they are retaken (`world_state.py:4823-4833`); any attacker win adds +3 `battle_win` (`combat_executor.py:4242-4243`), the rule EB-4.3 ("Europe fears the conqueror, not the defender") applied only to the defender role (`:4276-4285`), so driving an invader out of a French homeland province alarms Europe. **Sightings:** conquest #24 (P3, turn 14, action 269, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0269.jpg`); diplomacy-first #26 (P3, turn 8, action 202, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0202.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A capture of a province France holds title to, and a win in a province France owns, add no threat; the result line names any threat it does add and why. |
| **DDB-43** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB43`; Traced on the record (the conquest run kept no save — a live client session): a province changes controller between two other courts by a capture (`capture_region`, the enemy phase — the record shows Prussia's corps taking provinces in the Fourth Coalition's war while France's corps stood in Franconia), by the reckless auto-charge's mirrored conquest, or by a treaty cession; only an ask-back return (`province_returned`) was ever announced. `announce_third_party_capture` queues "X passes from A to B" and, for our corps standing there when its entry is closed, "Bernadotte stands on Franconia, now Prussia's soil — we are at peace and hold no open borders: he may march out, but no corps of ours may enter it again"; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB43` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=living_balance⟩ | **A province passing between other courts goes unannounced, and corps on soil that closed are not told.** Partly traced: entry under ARMISTICE is refused by the movement law (`movement_executor.py:453-486`, `diplomacy.py:10454-10480`); the corps had retreated into Franconia while it was Bavaria's; how it passed to Prussia was not traced, and no dispatch line or map cue marked the change. **Sightings:** conquest #30 (P3, turn 18, action 338, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0338.jpg`). **Decided fix:** Traced first (S13b's own probe on the conquest save); then a province changing hands between other courts is a dispatch line, and corps standing on soil whose entry just closed are told so. |
| **DDB-44** | P3 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`cd8f3210`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB44`; `movement_executor.supply_warning_line` (the turn's own bill, `WorldState.supply_forecast`) on a move, a march's first step and its turn reports; the sighting's re-play is DD-B's re-measure, S14e, which reads every road again); was: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_field.py::TestDDB44` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=economy⟩ | **A march into a crowded province gives no supply warning; the shortage strikes at turn start.** Supply attrition is charged at the turn's tick (`world_state.py:7888-7951`); its forecast exists only in the battle muster (`combat_executor.py:2408-2454`), never on a move. **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #7 (P3, turn 2, action 49, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0049.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A move or march into a province whose supply the stack outruns says so in its reply, with the forecast loss. |
| **DDB-45** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-4 (the voice); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_voice.py::TestDDB45` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=command⟩ | **A two-part question is read as one, and answered wrong or not at all.** Every classifier reads a line as ONE question: in "how strong is Mack and where is Vienna" the how-many pattern captures "Mack and where is Vienna", finds two names and returns None (`question_desk.py:116-149`, `:213-215`); the state desk then builds `enemy_at` with place = Vienna and prints "No, Sire — Mack stands at Swabia, not Vienna" (`state_desk.py:596-600`, `:2353-2357`); "where is Lannes and who holds Bohemia" fails the same way to the shrug. **Sightings:** conquest #1 (P3, turn 1, action 5, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0005.jpg`); conquest #8 (P3, turn 3, action 60, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0060.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A line joining two questions (" and " + where / who / how / what) is split and each part answered; `enemy_at` sets a place only when "at / in <place>" follows the foe. |
| **DDB-46** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-4 (the voice); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_voice.py::TestDDB46` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=narration⟩ | **The notice rail keeps stale cards, and old events lead it forever.** State cards are never retired when the state ends: the deficit card on recovery (`world_state.py:8185-8189`), the brewing card when the coalition forms (`coalition.py:3328-3344`); one-shot event notices posted at CRITICAL ("orders lost", `combat_executor.py:5536-5543`; "Armistice Collapsed", `diplomacy.py:11047-11052`) are never evicted by the cap and sort first (`notifications.py:405-408`, `:440-444`; `notification_bar.gd:396-406`). **Sightings:** conquest #9 (P3, turn 4, action 74, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0074.jpg`); conquest #33 (P3, turn 19, action 354, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0354.jpg`); the sea #8 (P3, turn 3, action 64, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0064.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A state card is dismissed when its state ends; an event notice posts at HIGH and ages out after its turn. |
| **DDB-47** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-4 (the voice); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_voice.py::TestDDB47` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=command⟩ | **"… to join Ney and Davout" is split into a second order.** `_AND_MARSHAL_SPLIT_RE_TEMPLATE` is `\s+and\s+(?={names}\b)`: it splits at "and <our marshal>" even when no verb follows (`parser.py:205-207`, `:549-555`), against its own comment's shape "and <Marshal>, <verb>". **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #6 (P3, turn 1, action 41, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0041.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The split requires an order verb after the name, as the comma template does (`parser.py:211`). |
| **DDB-48** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-4 (the voice); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_voice.py::TestDDB48` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=command⟩ | **"How do I …?" dumps the whole command reference instead of answering.** Any "how do / can I …" matches `_SYNTAX_QUESTION_RE` and is sent to the full manual on purpose (`llm_client.py:960-973`, `:3175-3182`; `meta_executor.py:864-883`); the manual has no section for ports, the Continental System, fleets' postures or readiness; "help" matches exact phrases only, so "help navy" falls to the generic "this order eludes me" (`llm_client.py:2440`, `:3473`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #8 (P3, turn 2, action 59, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0059.jpg`); diplomacy-first #21 (P3, turn 5, action 161, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0161.jpg`); the sea #1 (P3, turn 1, action 8, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0008.jpg`); the sea #5 (P3, turn 1, action 26, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0026.jpg`); the sea #9 (P3, turn 3, action 67, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0067.jpg`); the sea #22 (P3, turn 12, action 308, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0308.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A "how do / can I <verb> …" question is answered by the manual's ONE section its nouns name (fleet / blockade / readiness → the naval section; several marshals → the support and muster section), and "help <topic>" prints one section; the manual gains a naval-and-ports section. |
| **DDB-49** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-4 (the voice); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_voice.py::TestDDB49` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=command⟩ | **The command reference carries a developer's note and a stale Congress count.** `meta_executor.py:1081-1082` ("three instruments that shipped and were never named here, so no player could learn they exist") and a hardcoded "50 TITLED" (`:1105-1106`) while `congress.HOLD_TITLED` is 45 (`congress.py:90`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #8 (P3, turn 2, action 59, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0059.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The developer's line goes; the count is read from `congress.hold_titled(world)`. |
| **DDB-50** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-4 (the voice); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_voice.py::TestDDB50` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=narration⟩ | **A captured enemy marshal released by a peace returns to the field unannounced.** The peace with Austria released Mack (`diplomacy.py:2803-2807`), but the release event is stamped with Austria's nation and the dispatch drops foreign events (`world_state.py:5843-5856`, `dispatch.py:5614-5625`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #33 (P3, turn 18, action 288, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0288.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A release of a marshal France held prints in the ratification's summary and the dispatch. |
| **DDB-51** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-4 (the voice); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_voice.py::TestDDB51` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=narration⟩ | **A war purpose against an eliminated court is still printed.** Elimination sets PEACE without `cleanup_war_end` (`world_state.py:5025-5033`, its own comment says so), so the court's war objective is never concluded and the dispatch keeps its War Purpose line (`dispatch.py:6530-6533`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #33 (P3, turn 18, action 288, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0288.jpg`). **Decided fix:** Elimination concludes the court's war objectives. |
| **DDB-52** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-4 (the voice); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_voice.py::TestDDB52` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=living_balance⟩ | **Europe's alarm "holds" at 45 while the shown sum says it falls.** The Armed Peace clamps the decay so the alarm never falls below `ARMED_PEACE_WATCH` = 45 while France holds the bloc (`coalition.py:117`, `:1459-1486`); the forecast nets 0 ("holding") but `alarm_road` prints the gross decay and never names the watch (`congress.py:343-362`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #34 (P3, turn 20, action 295, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0295.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The road prints the decay held and why: "the Armed Peace holds it at 45 while our bloc is a third of Europe". |
| **DDB-53** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-4 (the voice); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_voice.py::TestDDB53` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=narration⟩ | **One siege prints three contradicting dispatch lines (destroyed, holds, taken).** Duplicates are matched on (class, identity): `garrison_held`, `garrison_stormed` and `region_lost` for one province are different classes, so all three print, sorted by weight rather than time (`dispatch.py:2538-2562`, `:840-846`, `:3288-3310`). **Sightings:** the sea #25 (P3, turn 15, action 337, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0337.jpg`). **Decided fix:** One province's garrison events collapse into its latest outcome; the Congress count's change names the title lost. |
| **DDB-54** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-5 (the client); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_client.py::TestDDB54` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=ui_ux⟩ | **After a typed order, the first Escape only leaves the command line; a second closes the screen.** While the command line has focus its Escape arm releases focus and consumes the event (`main.gd:1270-1277`), so `_unhandled_input`'s close-the-screen ladder (`:1477-1486`) never sees it. **Sightings:** conquest #25 (P3, turn 15, action 291, `docs/audits/dd_b/conquest/frames/0291.jpg`); the sea #28 (P3, turn 20, action 405, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0405.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The command line's Escape runs the same ladder first (the wizard, an open screen, the region panel, the pinned screen) and only then releases focus. |
| **DDB-55** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-5 (the client); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_client.py::TestDDB55` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=ui_ux⟩ | **The title screen's caption names the next painting while the last one still shows.** The caption is set when the 2.8 s crossfade starts, while the outgoing painting, drawn on top, is still nearly opaque (`main_menu.gd:743-749`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #1 (P3, the title screen, action 1, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0001.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The caption changes at the crossfade's midpoint. |
| **DDB-56** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-5 (the client); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_client.py::TestDDB56` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=ui_ux⟩ | **An ally's lost province is announced in the colour of good news.** A capture is coloured red only when taken from France; an ally's loss uses `COLOR_CONQUEST` (green) (`enemy_phase_dialog.gd:389`, `:882-890`). **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #7 (P3, turn 2, action 49, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0049.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A capture from France or an ally reads red; the backend stamps the alignment as it does for battles. |
| **DDB-57** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-5 (the client); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_client.py::TestDDB57` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=ui_ux⟩ | **The command window shows about four lines and a wheel notch barely moves it: a long reply cannot be read.** The window's default height is 0.32 of the viewport less its header and input row (`main.tscn:220-233`, `ui_settings.gd:212-218`); `main.gd` sets no wheel step, so a notch moves a fraction of a small page; the help is one block of about 9,500 px (`meta_executor.py:884+`). **Sightings:** the sea #5 (P3, turn 1, action 26, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0026.jpg`). **Decided fix:** A wheel notch moves three whole lines; a reply taller than the window opens at its first line with the window raised to its tallest snap until the next command. |
| **DDB-58** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-6 (the sea); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_sea.py::TestDDB58` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=naval⟩ | **The Admiralty counts the closed ports but never says how a port closes or which are open.** The payload carries the share, the next notch and the members (`strategic_ledger.gd:940-969`; `naval.py:3481-3515`, `:1310-1350`) but no sentence on what closes a port (its owner at war with Britain, its satellite, or a System member) and no list of the open ones. **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #22 (P3, turn 5, action 163, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0163.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The Admiralty states the rule in one sentence and lists the open courts with their ports. |
| **DDB-59** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-6 (the sea); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_sea.py::TestDDB59` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=naval⟩ | **The Admiralty's "pooling a fleet" and "staging the camp" read as orders; both are automatic states.** Pooling is no order: an ally at war with the same foe adds 80% of her sail on her own (`naval.py:302-303`, `:429-462`), yet the legend says a crossing "opens by building or pooling a fleet" (`naval.py:3539-3542`, `:1669`); "staged" means two consecutive end-of-turn checks with 40,000 in the camp while at war with the island power, reset by any truce (`naval.py:2566-2600`, `:2235-2237`), and the ledger shows the men but never the count (`strategic_ledger.gd:917-924`). **Sightings:** the sea #4 (P3, turn 1, action 23, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0023.jpg`); the sea #13 (P3, turn 5, action 104, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0104.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The legend says an ally's sail already counts ("Spain's 30 sail already count"); the camp line reads "staged after 2 turns standing at war with Britain (1 of 2) — no order needed", and "no army is staged" says why. |
| **DDB-60** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-6 (the sea); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_sea.py::TestDDB60` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=naval⟩ | **Asked what postures the fleet can take, Berthier reports only its present one.** A fleet question with no court named goes to `_answer_fleet`, which reports the current status only (`state_desk.py:539-540`, `:1589-1605`); the two postures (`naval.py:196`) are never named. **Sightings:** the sea #6 (P3, turn 1, action 27, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0027.jpg`). **Decided fix:** `_answer_fleet` names both postures, what each does and the order for each. |
| **DDB-61** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-6 (the sea); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_sea.py::TestDDB61` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=naval⟩ | **The Admiralty never says what stops the readiness rot, nor what a blockade order costs, before the order.** The keel receipt says the rot "is the number to fix" with no remedy (`naval_executor.py:83-98`); the remedy sentence exists but only in the Diversion forecast (`naval.py:2833-2851`, `_window_remedy`); the "Blockade the enemy" chip note lists only what it closes (`naval.py:3692-3714`), and the self-blockade rot and "our coasts go unguarded" are said only after the order is spent (`naval_executor.py:315-325`); a blockaded fleet may still take the blockade posture, so "pinned in port" and "stands out to sea" both print (`naval.py:3469`, `war_status.py:550`). **Sightings:** the sea #9 (P3, turn 3, action 67, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0067.jpg`); the sea #10 (P3, turn 3, action 68, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0068.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The rot line carries its remedy (`_window_remedy`); the chip note carries the self-blockade clause and the uncovered coast; a self-blockaded fleet's order reads "ordered to blockade stations — Britain still pins her". |
| **DDB-62** | P3 | OPEN — Pre-Deploy **S13b "DD-B fixes"**, slice S13b-6 (the sea); completion: the decided fix below holds, `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_sea.py::TestDDB62` pins it, and S13b's closing re-play on the DD-B instrument shows the sighting gone; STATUS: ▶ NEXT UP "S13b — DD-B fixes" ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=naval⟩ | **"Lay down a ship at Normandy" lays it at Bordelais.** A keel is nation-wide (`lay_down_ship` adds to the one fleet); the receipt always prints the first yard and the typed yard is never read (`naval_executor.py:173`, `:184`; `naval.py:3764-3765`) — the census's "disclosed", harmless to the fleet. **Sightings:** the sea #11 (P3, turn 4, action 88, `docs/audits/dd_b/sea/frames/0088.jpg`). **Decided fix:** The receipt echoes the named yard when it is one of ours, and says the fleet is one whichever yard lays it. |

**The rulings' builds and the siblings found while ruling** (October 14, 2026; `docs/audits/DD_B_RULINGS_2026_10_14.md`).

| **DDB-R1** | P2 | BUILT October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 3 (`ab6314a9`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_rulings.py::TestDDBR1`; `combat_executor.pursuit_ask`: a typed attack out of reach asks at no cost, naming the price and the first march; the answer re-issues the attack confirmed through CR-2's grammar (option `flags`); `SYSTEMS_REFERENCE.md` §118.2; the sighting's re-play is S14e's); was: the conquest line "Ney, attack Kutuzov in Moravia" typed with Ney in Bohemia asks, spends nothing and names the pursuit's price and first march; yes pursues with NPC-D4's terms, no spends nothing; the AI's and the strategic roads are unchanged; BASELINE_SERIES and M1–M7 byte-identical; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_rulings.py::TestDDBR1` pins it ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=command⟩ | **Build `DESIGN_REFINEMENT.md` DDB-D1's ruling — An attack on a marshal out of reach silently becomes a 2-AP pursuit that marches at once.** **Ruling:** ASK FIRST. Engaged means fight, stand or retreat: a free disengage by `move` would undercut the retreat's price and the AI's engagement rung. But `retreat` already falls back onto an ally's or a vassal's province (AAR-D4), so the refusal's "he must fight or stand" is false there: it builds its friendly list with `retreat_soil_is_friendly`, says "withdraw" and names the order and its price ("'Davout, retreat to Swabia' — free, −X% effectiveness, once a turn"). Display only. Its sibling: DDB-X3. **Sightings:** diplomacy-first #16 (P3, turn 4, action 113, `docs/audits/dd_b/diplomacy/frames/0113.jpg`). |

| **DDB-X9** | P2 | FIXED October 15, 2026 — Pre-Deploy S13b part 2 (`8f3f1141`; `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_rulings.py::TestDDBX9`; the letter's lines are the review's own reader (`settlement_status_quo_review` — the ratifier's `status_quo_forecast_lines` per covered cross-side pair, less what the package moves); the sea #18 board reads "Franche-Comte returns to us by the treaty" in both); was: the fix below holds and `tests/test_ddb_s13b_the_rulings.py::TestDDBX9` pins it ⟨SF step=pre-deploy S13 · DD-B · pillar=diplomacy⟩ | **A court's peace offer lists who keeps what by its own homeland-only rule, not the ratifier's.** Found by DDB-5 / DDB-12's sweep (every surface that states the status quo): `settlement_offers._derive_status_quo_lines` (`:2591-2622`, read at `:2853` and re-shown by `main.py:6825`) lists an occupier's hold on an enemy's STARTING homeland only, so a conquest titled outside a homeland goes unlisted and an ally's retaken homeland reads as kept, where the ratifier (`title_status_quo_retentions`, DDB-12's `homeland_returns`) titles it home. **Decided fix:** the letter's lines read the ratifier's own `status_quo_forecast_lines` per cross-side pair, as DDB-5's review does (`settlement_staging.settlement_status_quo_review`), less the provinces the package moves; the review and the letter agree on one board. |
